# Current production record: onset-entry cap, 27 September 2026

Current run: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/`, using seed 20260709,
20,000 draws per production cell and 128 midpoint integration points per branch.
The correction is fixed in `docs/prespec-addendum-2026-09-27-entry-cap.md`.
Priors, SEJ fits, matching conventions and other model elements are unchanged.
The headings and chronology below are historical; corrected rows state current
values first and retain the previous text after **v1.2:**. Unchanged rows retain
their existing values and correction history. The complete machine-readable
row index is `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`.
The complete comparison, including every changed map coordinate registered by
R100, is `trr/reviews/round13/capfix-diff.md` and the run's
`registered-number-diff.csv`. Differences below floating-point precision are ignored.

Scope limitation: R51/R63/R67/R87 retain the old linear-rate quotient as a
labelled diagnostic. Its frontier interpretation needs a separate prespecified
repair because entry timing now depends on the entry rate. Manuscript text and
the supplement generator were not edited in this job.

# Result-of-record registry (production dist `2020f2c_20260709`)

Every manuscript number resolves to a row here; `numeric_audit` gates the manuscript against
this file and `manuscript/refs/numeric-manifest.yml`. Producing artefacts:
`model/outputs/dist/2020f2c_20260709/{validity_gates,decision_summary}.json`,
`grid_results_{primary,optdev,pessdev}.csv`, `prcc_baseline_s1a.csv`,
`prcc_bootstrap_full.csv`, `calibration_curve.csv`, `decision_surface.npz`,
`speed_sweep.csv`, `sight_sweep.csv`, `baseline_traces.npz`. Priors frozen in
`model/config/priors.toml`; structure and rules in `docs/model-design.md` and
`docs/prespec.md`, both committed before the priors were valued (enforced by the run
guard). Baseline cell 300 veh/h/dir, 250 m section, 8-h operation day. Record bands from
`research/crash-record-bounds.md` (head-on 6e-6 to 2e-5 serious-harm events per
operation-day; central 1.2e-5) and `research/controller-strike-bounds.md` (controller
5e-6 to 2e-5; central 1e-5).

Source: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`, `R17`. **v1.2:** P(dH<0) at 20,000 iterations differs by 0.0007 between independent seeds; structural identities (dH reduces exactly to -W3 under behaviourally identical strategies; measured violation count preserved under re-timing) hold to machine precision; all 30 stable grid cells reported, none suppressed; 1000 veh/h/dir infeasible at every sampled headway |
| R18 | Cycle geometry facts used in prose | Encounter-free candidate-entry share using the effective post-cap candidate mixture, before sight weighting; not a fraction of elapsed red time. `encounter_free_candidate_entry_share` = 0.7133501247. Source: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`, `R18`. **v1.2:** Onset-runner protection: the all-red protects entries whose remaining clearance covers their own transit; safe-entry window 77.2% of the red at baseline. Violations concentrate at red onset (the protected window), which is why the measured violation rates do not carry the harm the raw rates suggest |

# Journal-version registry (production dist `e64d394_20260926`, 2026-09-26)

The journal version (Transportation Research Record) re-ran the whole suite from commit
`e64d394` with the attended-device arm S2 added under `docs/prespec.md` Addendum A. The three
frozen strategies reproduce dist `2020f2c_20260709` exactly (every S0, S1a and S1b column of
every grid cell identical; baseline traces bit for bit, pinned by
`model/tests/test_afad_s2.py`), so rows R1 to R18 stand unchanged and are cited as before.
Producing artefacts: `validity_gates.json`, `decision_summary.json` (S1a, regenerated),
`decision_summary_s2.json`, `model_form_sensitivity.csv` and `model_form_most_adverse.json`,
`welfare_summary.json`, `grid_results_{primary,optdev,pessdev}.csv`, `prcc_baseline_s2.csv`,
`prcc_bootstrap_s2.csv`, `calibration_curve_s2.csv`, `decision_surface_s2.npz`,
`speed_sweep.csv`, `sight_sweep.csv`, `baseline_traces.npz`. Record bands as above. Monetary
values from `model/config/welfare-values.toml` (MBCM v1.7.5, July 2021 prices).

| R50 | **Reads at New Zealand's own serious-harm head-on levels** (c = 1e-5; the S1 band tops at 2e-5) | `2.1e-05.S1a` = 0.33625; `2.1e-05.S1b` = 0.3848; `2.1e-05.S2` = 0.6218; `6.4e-05.S1a` = 0.1763; `6.4e-05.S1b` = 0.21195; `6.4e-05.S2` = 0.4725; `0.00013.S1a` = 0.10565; `0.00013.S1b` = 0.13; `0.00013.S2` = 0.36165. Source: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`, `R50`. **v1.2:** NZ serious+fatal head-on at roadworks: 168 of 883 crashes 1990-2025 are fatal or serious (19%), about 8.6 a year at the recent 45 a year, over 0.4M op-days = 2.1e-5 uncorrected (1.1e-5 to 5.7e-5 over the exposure range 0.15M to 0.75M); x3 to x6 work-zone under-reporting correction gives 6.4e-5 to 1.3e-4. P(dH < 0) S1a / S1b / S2: at h = 2.1e-5: 0.332 / 0.382 / 0.586; at 6.4e-5: 0.169 / 0.201 / 0.425; at 1.3e-4: 0.099 / 0.123 / 0.317. Great Britain serious+fatal over its exposure range: 7.6e-6 to 2.1e-5 (central 1.3e-5). The S1 band (6e-6 to 2e-5, central 1.2e-5) is therefore the British range; the NZ uncorrected central sits at its top and the corrected NZ level above it. |
| R51 | **Pre-registered frontier test and decision thresholds** (prespec: p* = 0.5 and 0.8; H's practical claim fails if the observed unattended-signal violation band lies mostly above the p* = 0.5 break-even frontier at baseline) | **Legacy linear-rate proxy only: the cap makes harm nonlinear in entry rate, so these regenerated quotients are not validated break-even frontiers.** `S1a.p5` = 0.29495905; `S1a.p20` = 1.625405759; `S1a.median` = 8.932256782; `S1a.p80` = 51.80909299; `S1a.p95` = 273.4275471; `S2.p5` = 0.6379458639; `S2.p20` = 3.313617691; `S2.median` = 18.81733428; `S2.p80` = 103.3302215; `S2.p95` = 498.0051354. Source: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`, `R51`. **v1.2:** Per-iteration break-even violation rate, elicited levels, per cent of facing vehicles: S1a median 12.2 (the p* = 0.5 frontier), 20th percentile 2.19 (the p* = 0.8 frontier), 80th 67.7; S2 18.8 / 3.31 / 103. Observed unattended-signal band 0.8 to 7.9 per cent (Table S3): below the p* = 0.5 frontier, so the p* = 0.5 test did not reject; mostly above the p* = 0.8 frontier, so high confidence was not reached at the elicited levels. At the record calibration the head-on level is set by the record and the violation rate drops out (R9: the W5-driven clause FAILED). |

The tree's implied collisions at signals (median 7.26e-4) are scaled to the collision record, so the implied serious harm at signals becomes 4.7e-7 per op-day at the central (1.4e-7 to 1.4e-6 across the band), 25 times below the serious-harm record. — Signals (S1a): queue tail at the panel's level (the plan's ledger) P = 0.828 central, corners 0.768 to 0.869, median dH -3.84e-5, within margin 0.440; queue tail excluded 0.902 (0.681 to 0.982), median dH -8.6e-6, within margin 0.914; queue tail record-scaled 0.904 (0.761 to 0.961). Monitored (S1b): 0.645 (0.562 to 0.710) / 0.919 (0.724 to 0.987) / 0.837 (0.674 to 0.917). Device (S2, identical under every queue-tail treatment): 0.800 (0.662 to 0.886), median dH -7.1e-6, within margin 0.919. P(S2 < S1a) on this axis, queue tail excluded: 0.28. The serious-harm axis used since v0.3 (R13) was adopted after the record checks, because both records the comparison turns on are in serious-harm units; the plan does not record that change. |
| R65 | **Changes made on 9 July 2026 after a first record-calibrated result had been seen** (git history, verified 2026-09-26: 3de8e5b at 19:00 rebuilt the manuscript on the conditioned model with the decision surface; 0affd85 at 22:57 amended the plan; 72987dc at 23:06 re-valued priors; the v0.3 results followed at 23:39). | 0affd85 added to the plan the equivalence bound (margin m = one event per 100 site-years, 4.0e-5), the value-of-information rule, the elicited-level disclosure, the mutual-sight variable d_sight_m and the sight-scaled definitions of w_occ and q_lead, and rebuilt the red-running tree with sight conditioning and green-gap arrivals. 72987dc re-valued the clearance-speed prior from uniform 30 to 36 km/h to uniform 20 to 40, the operating-speed prior from uniform 40 to 60 km/h to triangular 30, 40, 50, and added d_sight_m (log-uniform 150 to 3,000 m) to the contested list. The conference version carried these without stating their timing; the journal version states it in supplement S7. |

Record-scaled queue-tail difference median -8.9e-7 (was -1.3e-6). Supersedes those cells of R4, R30, R38, R52 and R61. |

operation day. Queue-tail differences and avoidance harm are excluded. The device
rows use median matching; both pathway denominators use means in the mean-matched
signal row. These are uncertainty intervals in expected rates, not day-to-day
variation. Full precision is retained in the audit JSON.

## Round 10 definition correction (version 1.2)

| ID | Result or input of record | Definition and existing source |
|---|---|---|
| R101 | Meaning of the unchanged 77.2% diagnostic in R8 and R18 | Encounter-free candidate-entry share using the effective post-cap candidate mixture, before sight weighting; not a fraction of elapsed red time. `encounter_free_candidate_entry_share` = 0.7133501247. Source: `model/outputs/dist/capfix_20260927/registered_reads.json`, `R101`. **v1.2:** Meaning of the unchanged 77.2% diagnostic in R8 and R18 (and the analogous S2 diagnostic in R21) — The saved baseline median of `diag_S1a_p_safe_window` is 0.7718400077831457, still 77% at manuscript precision. `conflict.w5_tree` accumulates the indicator that no opposing vehicle is inside and no next opposing entry occurs before exit, weighted by `w_onset / n_grid` for moving onset entries and `(1 - w_onset) / n_grid` for later standing entries. It is an encounter-free candidate-entry share under that mixture, before visible-occupancy entry weighting and its normalisation. It is neither the fraction of elapsed red time nor the fraction clearing before opposing release. The red is `2C + G_opp`; the first opposing crossing is `C + startup_lost_s`. This corrects the descriptions in R8, R18 and R21, not their values, saved outputs or implementation. |
| R102 | Existing conditioning priors brought into main Table 1 | `model/config/priors.toml`: visible-opponent entry multiplier `w_occ` log-uniform 0.0001 to 0.01; opposing-driver entry probability `q_lead` log-uniform 0.001 to 0.1; onset candidate share `w_onset` uniform 0.50 to 0.95. The first two are multiplied by separation divided by sight distance when the opponent is visible. All are unchanged, previously in Table S8 and indexed by R94. |

Round 10 display placement: the current signed-change display is main Figure 6,
operator exposure is Figure 7, and sight is Figure 8. The avoidance-model display
is reserved for supplement S8 by the round-10 request. The current build audit is
`trr/reviews/round10/v1.2-figure-audit.json`; its numerical maps, interval summaries
and seven underlying device rows agree with R100's historical audit. Only four
change rows and three device rows are displayed in the main paper.

## Model-form headline reads (version 1.2)

Source: `model/outputs/dist/e64d394_20260926/model_form_headline_reads.json`;
script: `model/scripts/model_form_headline_reads.py`, importing the unchanged
`model/scripts/review_reads.py` calculations. Primary shares reproduce R100 exactly,
and a primary rerun reproduces all 130 `baseline_traces.npz` arrays bit for bit.
Each form uses the same 20,000 paired draws, seed 20260709, 128-point integration
grid, baseline 300 veh/h/direction and 250 m section, and unchanged production
priors and per-expert SEJ fits. All 20,000 draws are finite for each headline.
Alternative traces use the baseline schema and are saved as
`model/outputs/dist/e64d394_20260926/model_form_traces_<form>.npz`, with form IDs
`common_cause_0p5`, `common_cause_1p0`, `threshold`, and `loglogistic`.

Values below are in this order: primary logistic in TTC, common cause 0.5,
common cause 1.0, hard threshold, logistic in ln(TTC). Each form's controller
and signal head-on terms are re-anchored on its own medians or means; tied-device
reads use median matching. Queue-tail differences and avoidance harm are excluded.
Most adverse means the lowest favourable share for each headline separately,
following A6's direction. The minimum across all five forms and, where different,
the minimum among the four alternatives are distinguished. These reads extend
R29's structural sensitivity to mean matching and the tied operator construction;
they do not replace the primary form or the earlier encroachment-device readings.
